#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+namespace {

+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * stride;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}

+void updateTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                const size_t columns, const size_t depth) {
+    const size_t fullRows = rows / 4 * 4;
+    const size_t fullColumns = columns / 8 * 8;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < fullRows; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += 8) {
+            updateMicroTile(c + i * stride + j, a + i * stride,
+                            bt + j, stride, depth);
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        const size_t start = i < fullRows ? fullColumns : 0;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            const double value = a[i * stride + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = start; j < columns; ++j) {
+                c[i * stride + j] -= value * bt[k * stride + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }

+void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transpose,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows, const size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[i * stride + k] * diagonal[j * stride + k];
+            }
+            panel[i * stride + j] =
+                (panel[i * stride + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * stride + j];
+        }
+    }
+    // The unused upper triangle provides scratch space for transposed panels.
+    // Readers depend on completion of this entire solve-and-transpose task.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            transpose[j * stride + i] = panel[i * stride + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace

+// Tiled right-looking Cholesky with a dependency graph instead of panel barriers.
+// The top-left address of each lower tile is its OpenMP dependency token.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* data = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> success{true};
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(success)
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
+                const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
+                double* diagonal = data + k * n + k;
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonal, n, width, k) \
+                    shared(success) depend(inout:diagonal[0])
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width, k)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
+                    double* transpose = data + k * n + i;
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width) \
+                        shared(success) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Submit the next diagonal and column first to expose lookahead.
+                for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t columns = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
+                    double* right = data + j * n + k;
+                    const double* transpose = data + k * n + j;
+                    for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                        double* left = data + i * n + k;
+                        double* tile = data + i * n + j;
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(tile, left, right, transpose, n, rows, columns, width) \
+                            shared(success) depend(in:left[0], right[0]) depend(inout:tile[0])
+                        {
+                            if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(tile, left, transpose, n, rows, columns, width);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
+            #pragma omp taskwait
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    return success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
 }

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {

+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * stride;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}

+// C -= A * B^T, including partial tiles at the matrix boundary.
+void updateTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                const size_t columns, const size_t depth) {
+    const size_t fullRows = rows / 4 * 4;
+    const size_t fullColumns = columns / 8 * 8;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < fullRows; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += 8) {
+            updateMicroTile(c + i * stride + j, a + i * stride,
+                            bt + j, stride, depth);
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        const size_t start = i < fullRows ? fullColumns : 0;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            const double value = a[i * stride + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = start; j < columns; ++j) {
+                c[i * stride + j] -= value * bt[k * stride + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * stride;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}

+void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transpose,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows, const size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[i * stride + k] * diagonal[j * stride + k];
+            }
+            panel[i * stride + j] =
+                (panel[i * stride + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * stride + j];
+        }

+    // Readers depend on completion of this entire solve-and-transpose task.

+    }

+// Tiled right-looking Cholesky with a dependency graph instead of panel barriers.
+// The top-left address of each lower tile is its OpenMP dependency token.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* data = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> success{true};
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(success)
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
+                const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
+                double* diagonal = data + k * n + k;
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonal, n, width, k) \
+                    shared(success) depend(inout:diagonal[0])
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width, k)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
+                    double* transpose = data + k * n + i;
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width) \
+                        shared(success) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Submit the next diagonal and column first to expose lookahead.
+                for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t columns = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
+                    double* right = data + j * n + k;
+                    const double* transpose = data + k * n + j;
+                    for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                        double* left = data + i * n + k;
+                        double* tile = data + i * n + j;
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(tile, left, right, transpose, n, rows, columns, width) \
+                            shared(success) depend(in:left[0], right[0]) depend(inout:tile[0])
+                        {
+                            if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(tile, left, transpose, n, rows, columns, width);
+                            }

+                    }

+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * stride;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}

+void updateTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                const size_t columns, const size_t depth) {
+    const size_t fullRows = rows / 4 * 4;
+    const size_t fullColumns = columns / 8 * 8;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < fullRows; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += 8) {
+            updateMicroTile(c + i * stride + j, a + i * stride,
+                            bt + j, stride, depth);
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        const size_t start = i < fullRows ? fullColumns : 0;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            const double value = a[i * stride + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = start; j < columns; ++j) {
+                c[i * stride + j] -= value * bt[k * stride + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }

+void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transpose,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows, const size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {

+// Tiled right-looking Cholesky with a dependency graph instead of panel barriers.
+// The top-left address of each lower tile is its OpenMP dependency token.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* data = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> success{true};

+    #pragma omp parallel shared(success)

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * stride;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}

+void updateTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                const size_t columns, const size_t depth) {
+    const size_t fullRows = rows / 4 * 4;
+    const size_t fullColumns = columns / 8 * 8;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < fullRows; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += 8) {
+            updateMicroTile(c + i * stride + j, a + i * stride,

+        }

+void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transpose,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows, const size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[i * stride + k] * diagonal[j * stride + k];
+            }
+            panel[i * stride + j] =
+                (panel[i * stride + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * stride + j];
+        }
+    }

+    // Readers depend on completion of this entire solve-and-transpose task.

+        }
+    }

+// Tiled right-looking Cholesky with a dependency graph instead of panel barriers.
+// The top-left address of each lower tile is its OpenMP dependency token.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* data = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> success{true};
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(success)
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
+                const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
+                double* diagonal = data + k * n + k;
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonal, n, width, k) \
+                    shared(success) depend(inout:diagonal[0])
+                {

+                        !factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width, k)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);

+                    double* transpose = data + k * n + i;

+                        shared(success) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+

+    return success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);

+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];

+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];

+// C -= A * B^T, including partial tiles at the matrix boundary.
+void updateTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                const size_t columns, const size_t depth) {
+    const size_t fullRows = rows / 4 * 4;
+    const size_t fullColumns = columns / 8 * 8;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < fullRows; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += 8) {
+            updateMicroTile(c + i * stride + j, a + i * stride,
+                            bt + j, stride, depth);

+void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transpose,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows, const size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[i * stride + k] * diagonal[j * stride + k];
+            }
+            panel[i * stride + j] =
+                (panel[i * stride + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * stride + j];
+        }
+    }
+    // The unused upper triangle provides scratch space for transposed panels.
+    // Readers depend on completion of this entire solve-and-transpose task.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            transpose[j * stride + i] = panel[i * stride + j];
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Tiled right-looking Cholesky with a dependency graph instead of panel barriers.
+// The top-left address of each lower tile is its OpenMP dependency token.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* data = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> success{true};
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(success)
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
+                const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
+                double* diagonal = data + k * n + k;
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonal, n, width, k) \
+                    shared(success) depend(inout:diagonal[0])
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width, k)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
+                    double* transpose = data + k * n + i;
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width) \
+                        shared(success) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Submit the next diagonal and column first to expose lookahead.
+                for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t columns = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
+                    [[maybe_unused]] double* right = data + j * n + k;
+                    const double* transpose = data + k * n + j;
+                    for (size_t i = j; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                        const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                        double* left = data + i * n + k;
+                        double* tile = data + i * n + j;
+                        #pragma omp task firstprivate(tile, left, right, transpose, n, rows, columns, width) \
+                            shared(success) depend(in:left[0], right[0]) depend(inout:tile[0])
+                        {
+                            if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(tile, left, transpose, n, rows, columns, width);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
+            #pragma omp taskwait
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    return success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
 }

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    // Unrolling this loop would spill the eight SIMD accumulators on AVX2 CPUs.
+    #pragma GCC unroll 1
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * stride;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}
+
+// C -= A * B^T, including partial tiles at the matrix boundary.
+void updateTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                const size_t columns, const size_t depth) {
+    const size_t fullRows = rows / 4 * 4;
+    const size_t fullColumns = columns / 8 * 8;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < fullRows; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += 8) {
+            updateMicroTile(c + i * stride + j, a + i * stride,
+                            bt + j, stride, depth);
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        const size_t start = i < fullRows ? fullColumns : 0;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            const double value = a[i * stride + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = start; j < columns; ++j) {
+                c[i * stride + j] -= value * bt[k * stride + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

#include <vector>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L"'^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu'"\\n\", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B"'^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B''^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L''^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L''^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e'"\\n\", maxError);
    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");
    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if ("'!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed'"\\n\");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#undef choleskyDecomposition
#undef generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix
#undef validateCholesky
#undef printUsage
#undef main
#include <omp.h>
#include <cassert>
#include <limits>

int main() {
    omp_set_dynamic(0);
    omp_set_num_threads(4);
    for (size_t n : {size_t(0), 1ul, 2ul, 5ul, 31ul, 63ul, 64ul, 65ul,
                      127ul, 128ul, 129ul, 257ul, 511ul, 512ul, 513ul}) {
        std::vector<double> input(n*n), generated(n*n);
        referenceGenerate(input, n);
        generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(generated, n);
        double generationError = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < input.size(); ++i) {
            generationError = std::max(generationError, fabs(input[i]-generated[i]));
        }
        assert(generationError < 1e-10);
        auto expected = input;
        assert(referenceCholesky(expected, n));
        for (int threads : {1, 2, 4, 8}) {
            omp_set_num_threads(threads);
            auto actual = input;
            assert(choleskyDecomposition(actual, n));
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                    assert(std::isfinite(actual[i*n+j]));
                    assert(fabs(actual[i*n+j]-expected[i*n+j]) <
                           1e-11 * (1.0 + fabs(expected[i*n+j])));
                    if (j > i) assert(actual[i*n+j] == 0.0);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    puts(\"Full factors match the original on 60 size/thread cases.\");
    for (size_t n : {1ul, 65ul, 129ul}) {
        std::vector<double> input(n*n, 0.0);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) input[i*n+i] = 1.0;
        input[n*n-1] = -1.0;
        for (int threads : {1, 4, 8}) {
            omp_set_num_threads(threads);
            auto actual = input;
            assert("'!choleskyDecomposition(actual, n));
        }
    }
    puts("Non-positive-definite matrices rejected across tile boundaries.");
    for (size_t n : {512ul, 1024ul, 2048ul}) {
        std::vector<double> input(n*n);
        omp_set_num_threads(8);
        generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(input, n);
        auto reference = input;
        auto begin = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        assert(referenceCholesky(reference, n));
        double baseline = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
                          std::chrono::steady_clock::now()-begin).count();
        printf("BENCH n=%zu original=%.3f ms'"\\n\", n, baseline);
        for (int threads : {1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32}) {
            omp_set_num_threads(threads);
            std::vector<double> times;
            for (int repeat = 0; repeat < 5; ++repeat) {
                auto actual = input;
                begin = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
                assert(choleskyDecomposition(actual, n));
                times.push_back(std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
                                std::chrono::steady_clock::now()-begin).count());
                assert(fabs(actual.back()-reference.back()) < 1e-10);
            }
            std::sort(times.begin(), times.end());
            printf(\"BENCH n=%zu threads=%d median=%.3f ms speedup=%.2f\\n\",
                   n, threads, times[2], baseline/times[2]);
            fflush(stdout);
        }
    }
}

+namespace {

+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    // Unrolling this loop would spill the eight SIMD accumulators on AVX2 CPUs.
+    #pragma GCC unroll 1
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * stride;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}
+
+// C -= A * B^T, including partial tiles at the matrix boundary.
+void updateTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                const size_t columns, const size_t depth) {
+    const size_t fullRows = rows / 4 * 4;
+    const size_t fullColumns = columns / 8 * 8;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < fullRows; i += 4) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += 8) {
+            updateMicroTile(c + i * stride + j, a + i * stride,
+                            bt + j, stride, depth);
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        const size_t start = i < fullRows ? fullColumns : 0;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+            const double value = a[i * stride + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = start; j < columns; ++j) {
+                c[i * stride + j] -= value * bt[k * stride + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transpose,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows, const size_t width) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[i * stride + k] * diagonal[j * stride + k];
+            }
+            panel[i * stride + j] =
+                (panel[i * stride + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * stride + j];
+        }
+    }
+    // The unused upper triangle provides scratch space for transposed panels.
+    // Readers depend on completion of this entire solve-and-transpose task.
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+            transpose[j * stride + i] = panel[i * stride + j];
+        }
+    }

+// Tiled right-looking Cholesky with a dependency graph instead of panel barriers.
+// The top-left address of each lower tile is its OpenMP dependency token.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    double* data = A.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> success{true};
+
+    #pragma omp parallel shared(success)
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += tileSize) {
+                const size_t width = std::min(tileSize, n - k);
+                double* diagonal = data + k * n + k;
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonal, n, width, k) \
+                    shared(success) depend(inout:diagonal[0])
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonal(diagonal, n, width, k)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t i = k + width; i < n; i += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t rows = std::min(tileSize, n - i);
+                    double* panel = data + i * n + k;
+                    double* transpose = data + k * n + i;
+                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width) \
+                        shared(success) depend(in:diagonal[0]) depend(inout:panel[0])
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solvePanel(panel, diagonal, transpose, n, rows, width);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Submit the next diagonal and column first to expose lookahead.
+                for (size_t j = k + width; j < n; j += tileSize) {
+                    const size_t columns = std::min(tileSize, n - j);
+                    [[maybe_unused]] double* right = data + j * n + k;
+                    const double* transpose = data + k * n + j;

+                        double* tile = data + i * n + j;

+                            shared(success) depend(in:left[0], right[0]) depend(inout:tile[0])
+                        {
+                            if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                updateTile(tile, left, transpose, n, rows, columns, width);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }

-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {

+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    // Unrolling this loop would spill the eight SIMD accumulators on AVX2 CPUs.
+    #pragma GCC unroll 1
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * tileSize;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}

+namespace {

+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;

+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    // Unrolling this loop would spill the eight SIMD accumulators on AVX2 CPUs.
+    #pragma GCC unroll 1
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * tileSize;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
+}

+void updateTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                const size_t columns, const size_t depth) {
+    // Packing avoids cache-set conflicts and TLB misses from the matrix stride.
+    alignas(64) double packed[tileSize * tileSize];
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        std::copy_n(bt + k * stride, columns, packed + k * tileSize);

+void solvePanel(double* panel, const double* diagonal, double* transpose,

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+
+// Keep a 4-by-8 output block in registers while streaming the two panels.
+// The right panel is transposed, so SIMD lanes access adjacent columns.
+inline void updateMicroTile(double* c, const double* a, const double* bt,
+                            const size_t stride, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c0[j] = c[j];
+        c1[j] = c[stride + j];
+        c2[j] = c[2 * stride + j];
+        c3[j] = c[3 * stride + j];
+    }
+    // Unrolling this loop would spill the eight SIMD accumulators on AVX2 CPUs.
+    #pragma GCC unroll 1
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        const double a1 = a[stride + k];
+        const double a2 = a[2 * stride + k];
+        const double a3 = a[3 * stride + k];
+        const double* b = bt + k * tileSize;
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+            c0[j] -= a0 * b[j];
+            c1[j] -= a1 * b[j];
+            c2[j] -= a2 * b[j];
+            c3[j] -= a3 * b[j];
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
+        c[j] = c0[j];
+        c[stride + j] = c1[j];
+        c[2 * stride + j] = c2[j];
+        c[3 * stride + j] = c3[j];
+    }
